90:     # 通过现有浏览器会话创建客户端，自动收集 cookie 并读取 User-Agent 组装默认请求头。
91:     @classmethod
92:     async def create(
93:         cls,
94:         *,
95:         page: Page,
96:         browser_context: BrowserContext,
97:         timeout: float,
98:         verify_ssl: bool,
99:     ) -> "DouyinClient":
100:         """基于现有浏览器会话创建客户端。
101: 
102:         从浏览器上下文收集 cookie，从页面读取真实 User-Agent 组装默认请求头。
103: 
104:         参数：
105:             page: 已打开抖音站点的 Playwright 页面。
106:             browser_context: 浏览器上下文。
107:             timeout: HTTP 请求超时时间（秒）。
108:             verify_ssl: 是否校验 SSL 证书。
109: 
110:         返回：
111:             初始化完成的 DouyinClient。
112:         """
113:         cookie_string, cookie_dict = await browser_cookies(
114:             browser_context, cls.cookie_urls
115:         )
116:         user_agent = str(await evaluate_stable(page, "() => navigator.userAgent"))
117:         return cls(
118:             page=page,
119:             headers={
120:                 "User-Agent": user_agent,
121:                 "Cookie": cookie_string,
122:                 "Host": "www.douyin.com",
123:                 "Origin": "https://www.douyin.com/",
124:                 "Referer": "https://www.douyin.com/",
125:                 "Content-Type": "application/json;charset=UTF-8",
126:             },
127:             cookie_dict=cookie_dict,
128:             timeout=timeout,
129:             verify_ssl=verify_ssl,
130:         )
131: 
132:     # 关闭底层 HTTP 连接。
133:     async def close(self) -> None:
134:         """关闭底层 HTTP 连接。"""
135:         await self.http.aclose()
136: 
137:     # 发送 HTTP 请求并校验响应为 JSON 对象。
138:     async def request(self, method: str, url: str, **kwargs: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
139:         """发送 HTTP 请求并校验响应为 JSON 对象。
140: 
141:         参数：
142:             method: HTTP 方法。
143:             url: 完整请求地址。
144: 
145:         返回：
146:             响应 JSON（保证为 dict）。
147: 
148:         异常：
149:             DataFetchError: 网络错误、响应为空/被拦截、或响应不是 JSON 对象时抛出。
150:         """
151:         started = time.monotonic()
152:         entry = DouyinRequestLogEntry(
153:             method=method,
154:             path=urlsplit(url).path,
155:             url=url,
156:             query_params=(
157:                 dict(kwargs["params"]) if isinstance(kwargs.get("params"), dict) else {}
158:             ),
159:             request_headers=dict(
160:                 kwargs.get("headers") or getattr(self, "headers", None) or {}
161:             ),
162:             request_body=(
163:                 dict(kwargs["data"]) if isinstance(kwargs.get("data"), dict) else None
164:             ),
165:             response_status=None,
166:             duration_ms=0,
167:             error=None,
168:         )
169:         try:
170:             try:
171:                 response: httpx.Response | None = None
172:                 for attempt in range(3):
173:                     try:
174:                         response = await self.http.request(method, url, **kwargs)
175:                         entry.response_status = response.status_code
176:                         response.raise_for_status()
177:                         break
178:                     except httpx.TransportError:
179:                         if attempt == 2:
180:                             raise
181:                         await asyncio.sleep(0.25 * (attempt + 1))
182:                 if response is None:  # pragma: no cover - 防御性保护
183:                     raise httpx.RequestError("抖音请求未返回响应")
184:             except httpx.HTTPError as exc:
185:                 # HTTPX 异常会保留完整签名后的请求 URL。此处切断异常链，
186:                 # 确保 msToken/a_bogus 永远不会进入 traceback 日志。
187:                 entry.error = type(exc).__name__
188:                 if isinstance(exc, httpx.HTTPStatusError):
189:                     entry.failure_detail = self._failure_detail_from_response(
190:                         exc.response
191:                     )
192:                 else:
193:                     entry.failure_detail = {
194:                         "kind": "transport_error",
195:                         "exception_type": type(exc).__name__,
196:                         "message": "网络请求未收到 HTTP 响应",
197:                     }
198:                 raise DataFetchError(f"抖音请求失败: {type(exc).__name__}") from None
199:             body = response.text
200:             if not body or body == "blocked":
201:                 entry.error = "blocked" if body == "blocked" else "empty"
202:                 entry.failure_detail = self._failure_detail_from_response(response)
203:                 raise DataFetchError(
204:                     f"抖音请求被拒绝: status={response.status_code}, body={'empty' if not body else 'blocked'}"
205:                 )
206:             try:
207:                 payload = response.json()
208:             except ValueError as exc:
209:                 entry.error = "non-json"
210:                 entry.failure_detail = self._failure_detail_from_response(response)
211:                 raise DataFetchError(
212:                     f"抖音响应不是 JSON: status={response.status_code}, length={len(response.content)}"
213:                 ) from exc
214:             if not isinstance(payload, dict):
215:                 entry.error = "non-object"
216:                 entry.failure_detail = self._failure_detail_from_response(response)
217:                 raise DataFetchError("抖音响应 JSON 顶层不是对象")
218:             business_status = payload.get("status_code")
219:             if business_status not in (None, 0, "0"):
220:                 normalized_status = str(business_status)[:32]
221:                 entry.error = f"DouyinBusinessError:{normalized_status}"
222:                 entry.failure_detail = self._failure_detail_from_response(response)
223:                 raise DataFetchError(
224:                     f"抖音接口业务状态失败: status_code={normalized_status}"
225:                 )
226:             return payload
227:         finally:
228:             entry.duration_ms = int((time.monotonic() - started) * 1000)
229:             await self._emit_request_log(entry)
230: 
231:     # 发送带公共参数与签名的 GET 请求。
232:     async def get(
233:         self,
234:         uri: str,
235:         params: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
236:         headers: dict[str, str] | None = None,
237:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
238:         """发送带公共参数与签名的 GET 请求。
239: 
240:         参数：
241:             uri: 接口路径（自动拼接主站 host）。
242:             params: 查询参数。
243:             headers: 自定义请求头，缺省使用客户端默认请求头。
244: 
245:         返回：
246:             响应 JSON 字典。
247:         """
248:         request_headers = headers or self.headers
249:         request_params = await self._process_params(
250:             uri, dict(params or {}), request_headers
251:         )
252:         return await self.request(
253:             "GET", f"{self.host}{uri}", params=request_params, headers=request_headers
254:         )
255: 
256:     # 发送带公共参数与签名的 POST 请求。
257:     async def post(
258:         self,
259:         uri: str,
260:         data: dict[str, Any],
261:         headers: dict[str, str] | None = None,
262:         params: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
263:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
264:         """发送带公共参数与签名的 POST 请求。
265: 
266:         参数：
267:             uri: 接口路径（自动拼接主站 host）。
268:             data: 表单请求体；params 为 None 时同时作为签名依据。
269:             headers: 自定义请求头，缺省使用客户端默认请求头。
270:             params: 查询参数；提供时仅对它做签名，data 原样作为请求体。
271: 
272:         返回：
273:             响应 JSON 字典。
274:         """
275:         request_headers = headers or self.headers
276:         signing_params = dict(params if params is not None else data)
277:         signed_params = await self._process_params(uri, signing_params, request_headers)
278:         request_kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
279:             "data": signed_params if params is None else data,
280:             "headers": request_headers,
281:         }
282:         if params is not None:
283:             request_kwargs["params"] = signed_params
284:         return await self.request("POST", f"{self.host}{uri}", **request_kwargs)
285: 
286:     # 检测当前会话的抖音登录状态。
287:     async def pong(
288:         self, browser_context: BrowserContext, require_self_profile: bool = False
289:     ) -> bool:
290:         """检测当前会话的抖音登录状态。
291: 
292:         require_self_profile 为 False 时，先查页面 localStorage 的 HasUserLogin
293:         与 cookie 的 LOGIN_STATUS 做快速判断，未命中再调用本人资料接口兜底；
294:         为 True 时跳过本地快速检查，直接调用接口校验。
295: 
296:         参数：
297:             browser_context: 浏览器上下文。
298:             require_self_profile: 是否强制通过本人资料接口校验登录状态。
299: 
300:         返回：
301:             已登录返回 True，否则 False。
302:         """
303:         if not require_self_profile:
304:             try:
305:                 local_storage = await self.page.evaluate("() => window.localStorage")
306:             except Exception:
307:                 local_storage = {}
308:             if (
309:                 isinstance(local_storage, dict)
310:                 and local_storage.get("HasUserLogin") == "1"
311:             ):
312:                 return True
313:             _, cookies = await browser_cookies(browser_context, self.cookie_urls)
314:             if cookies.get("LOGIN_STATUS") == "1":
315:                 return True
316:         try:
317:             response = await self.user_api.get_self_profile()
318:         except Exception:
319:             return False
320:         if response.get("status_code") not in (0, "0"):
321:             return False
322:         data = response.get("data")
323:         profile = (
324:             response.get("user")
325:             or response.get("user_info")
326:             or (data.get("user") if isinstance(data, dict) else None)
327:             or (data.get("user_info") if isinstance(data, dict) else None)
328:             or data
329:         )
330:         return isinstance(profile, dict) and bool(
331:             profile.get("uid") or profile.get("sec_uid") or profile.get("sec_user_id")
332:         )
333: 
334: 
335:         # 从浏览器上下文重新收集 cookie，并同步到请求头与 cookie_dict。
336: 
337:     # 更新客户端的 cookie 信息，从浏览器上下文中重新收集 cookie，并同步到请求头和 cookie_dict 中。
338:     async def update_cookies(self, browser_context: BrowserContext) -> None:
339:         """从浏览器上下文重新收集 cookie，并同步到请求头与 cookie_dict。"""
340:         cookie_string, cookie_dict = await browser_cookies(
341:             browser_context, self.cookie_urls
342:         )
343:         self.headers["Cookie"] = cookie_string
344:         self.cookie_dict = cookie_dict
345: 
346:     # 提取失败响应快照；正文预览在业务层落库前还会再次脱敏与限长。
347:     @staticmethod
348:     def _failure_detail_from_response(response: httpx.Response) -> dict[str, Any]:
349:         """提取失败响应快照；正文预览在业务层落库前还会再次脱敏与限长。"""
350:         detail: dict[str, Any] = {
351:             "http_status": response.status_code,
352:             "content_type": response.headers.get("content-type", ""),
353:         }
354:         if not response.content:
355:             detail["body"] = ""
356:             return detail
357:         try:
358:             detail["body"] = response.json()
359:         except ValueError:
360:             body = response.text
361:             detail["body"] = body[:8192]
362:             detail["truncated"] = len(body) > 8192
363:         return detail
364:

44: class DouyinInteractionExecutor:
45:     """通过已有 CDP 浏览器 profile 执行用户明确确认的写操作（评论/回复/私信）。
46: 
47:     只负责编排：把三种互动流程映射到浏览器会话与页面导航；页面级细节委托给
48:     ``PageController`` / ``CommentLocator`` / ``SubmitFlow`` / ``ResponseInspector``。
49:     """
50: 
51:     index_url = "https://www.douyin.com"  # 抖音主站地址
52:     interaction_page_marker = "mediacrawler:interaction"  # 自动化专用标签页标记（隔离其他标签页）
53: 
54:     def __init__(self, settings: Settings) -> None:
55:         """初始化执行器。
56: 
57:         参数：
58:             settings: 全局配置（超时、CDP 目录、远程槽位等）。
59:         """
60:         self.settings = settings
61: 
62:     async def execute(
63:         self,
64:         *,
65:         account: object | None = None,
66:         connection: InteractionBrowserConnection | None = None,
67:         request: InteractionExecutionRequest,
68:         step_callback: InteractionStepCallback | None = None,
69:     ) -> InteractionExecutionResult:
70:         """执行一次已确认的互动写操作（评论作品 / 回复评论 / 私信作者）。
71: 
72:         通过 CDP 连接账号浏览器、校验登录状态后，按互动类型分发执行；
73:         全程通过 step_callback 上报步骤进展。
74: 
75:         参数：
76:             account: 旧版账号对象（与 connection 二选一）。
77:             connection: 已解析的 CDP 连接参数（与 account 二选一）。
78:             request: 互动执行请求。
79:             step_callback: 步骤回调，可为 None。
80: 
81:         返回：
82:             互动执行结果。
83: 
84:         异常：
85:             InteractionExecutionError: 浏览器不可用、未登录或执行失败时抛出。
86:         """
87:         connection = self._execution_connection(
88:             account=account,
89:             connection=connection,
90:         )
91:         browser = CDPBrowserSession(
92:             self.settings,
93:             browser_mode=connection.browser_mode,
94:             remote_host=connection.remote_host,
95:             remote_port=connection.remote_port,
96:             user_data_dir=connection.user_data_dir,
97:             debug_port=connection.debug_port,
98:             reuse_existing_page=False,
99:             close_page_on_exit=False,
100:             page_marker=self.interaction_page_marker,
101:         )
102:         async with browser:
103:             if browser.page is None or browser.context is None:
104:                 raise InteractionExecutionError(
105:                     "browser_unavailable",
106:                     "CDP 浏览器页面不可用",
107:                     retryable=True,
108:                     affects_account_health=True,
109:                 )
110:             page = browser.page
111:             await page.bring_to_front()
112: 
113:             async def on_page_dialog_dismissed(dialog: Dialog) -> None:
114:                 """对话框被自动关闭后上报步骤（异常由 auto_dismiss_dialogs 吞掉）。"""
115:                 await self._trace(
116:                     step_callback,
117:                     page,
118:                     "page_dialog_dismissed",
119:                     f"已自动关闭网页对话框（{dialog.type}）",
120:                 )
121: 
122:             dialog_handler = dom.auto_dismiss_dialogs(
123:                 page, on_dismiss=on_page_dialog_dismissed
124:             )
125:             client: DouyinClient | None = None
126:             try:
127:                 try:
128:                     await page.goto(
129:                         self.index_url,
130:                         wait_until="domcontentloaded",
131:                         timeout=30_000,
132:                     )
133:                 except PlaywrightTimeoutError:
134:                     # 页面即使只加载了一部分，也已具备检测登录状态所需的会话信息。
135:                     pass
136:                 await self._trace(
137:                     step_callback,
138:                     page,
139:                     "browser_connected",
140:                     (
141:                         "已通过 CDP 连接账号浏览器并打开自动化专用标签页；"
142:                         f"已隔离 {browser.unrelated_page_count} 个其他标签页"
143:                     ),
144:                 )
145:                 client = await DouyinClient.create(
146:                     page=page,
147:                     browser_context=browser.context,
148:                     timeout=self.settings.DOUYIN_REQUEST_TIMEOUT,
149:                     verify_ssl=self.settings.DOUYIN_REQUEST_SSL_VERIFY,
150:                 )
151:                 if not await client.pong(browser.context, require_self_profile=True):
152:                     raise InteractionExecutionError(
153:                         "login_required",
154:                         "所选账号登录已失效，请先在账号管理中重新登录",
155:                         affects_account_health=True,
156:                     )
157:                 await client.update_cookies(browser.context)
158:                 await self._trace(
159:                     step_callback,
160:                     page,
161:                     "login_verified",
162:                     "账号登录状态验证通过",
163:                 )
164:                 if request.interaction_type == "video_comment":
165:                     return await self._comment_video(page, request, step_callback)
166:                 if request.interaction_type == "comment_reply":
167:                     return await self._reply_to_comment(
168:                         page, client, request, step_callback
169:                     )
170:                 return await self._message_creator(page, client, request, step_callback)
171:             except Exception as exc:
172:                 detail = (
173:                     f"执行失败：{exc}"
174:                     if isinstance(exc, InteractionExecutionError)
175:                     else "浏览器执行发生异常，已保留最后页面现场"
176:                 )
177:                 await self._trace(
178:                     step_callback,
179:                     page,
180:                     "execution_failed",
181:                     detail,
182:                 )
183:                 raise
184:             finally:
185:                 page.remove_listener("dialog", dialog_handler)
186:                 if client is not None:
187:                     await client.close()

189:     def _execution_connection(
190:         self,
191:         *,
192:         account: object | None,
193:         connection: InteractionBrowserConnection | None,
194:     ) -> InteractionBrowserConnection:
195:         """兼容历史 account 入参与中立的 connection DTO，解析出 CDP 连接参数。
196: 
197:         account 分支刻意使用结构化属性访问，使集成层不会重新引入
198:         对 ORM/应用层的依赖。
199: 
200:         异常：
201:             TypeError: account 与 connection 同时提供或同时缺失时抛出。
202:         """
203:         if connection is not None and account is not None:
204:             raise TypeError("account 和 connection 不能同时提供")
205:         if connection is not None:
206:             return connection
207:         if account is None:
208:             raise TypeError("必须提供 account 或 connection")
209:         return self._connection_from_legacy_account(account)
210: 
211:     def _connection_from_legacy_account(
212:         self, account: object
213:     ) -> InteractionBrowserConnection:
214:         """将旧版账号对象解析为 CDP 连接参数。
215: 
216:         local 模式按 profile_key 推导用户数据目录与调试端口；
217:         remote 模式读取槽位配置（缺省使用全局远程 CDP 地址）。
218: 
219:         异常：
220:             ValueError: 浏览器模式或槽位配置非法时抛出。
221:         """
222:         legacy_account = cast(_LegacyInteractionAccount, account)
223:         raw_mode = legacy_account.browser_mode
224:         mode = str(getattr(raw_mode, "value", raw_mode))
225:         if mode == "local":
226:             profile_root = (
227:                 self.settings.DOUYIN_CDP_USER_DATA_DIR.resolve().parent / "accounts"
228:             )
229:             return InteractionBrowserConnection(
230:                 browser_mode=mode,
231:                 user_data_dir=profile_root / str(legacy_account.profile_key),
232:                 debug_port=self.settings.DOUYIN_CDP_PORT
233:                 + (int(legacy_account.id.int) % 500),
234:             )
235:         if mode != "remote":
236:             raise ValueError(f"{mode!r} is not a valid DouyinBrowserMode")
237: 
238:         remote_slot = legacy_account.remote_slot
239:         if not remote_slot:
240:             return InteractionBrowserConnection(
241:                 browser_mode=mode,
242:                 remote_host=self.settings.DOUYIN_REMOTE_CDP_HOST,
243:                 remote_port=self.settings.DOUYIN_REMOTE_CDP_PORT,
244:             )
245:         slots = self._legacy_remote_slots()
246:         slot = slots.get(str(remote_slot))
247:         if slot is None:
248:             raise ValueError(f"远程浏览器槽位 {remote_slot} 未配置")
249:         host = str(slot.get("host") or "").strip()
250:         try:
251:             port = int(str(slot.get("port") or 0))
252:         except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
253:             raise ValueError("远程浏览���槽位端口无效") from exc
254:         if not host or not 1 <= port <= 65535:
255:             raise ValueError("远程浏览器槽位主机或端口无效")
256:         return InteractionBrowserConnection(
257:             browser_mode=mode,
258:             remote_host=host,
259:             remote_port=port,
260:         )
261: 
262:     def _legacy_remote_slots(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
263:         """解析 DOUYIN_REMOTE_CDP_SLOTS（JSON 对象）为槽位名到连接配置的映射。
264: 
265:         异常：
266:             ValueError: 配置不是有效 JSON 或格式非法时抛出。
267:         """
268:         raw = self.settings.DOUYIN_REMOTE_CDP_SLOTS.strip()
269:         if not raw:
270:             return {}
271:         try:
272:             payload = json.loads(raw)
273:         except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
274:             raise ValueError("DOUYIN_REMOTE_CDP_SLOTS 不是有效 JSON") from exc
275:         if not isinstance(payload, dict):
276:             raise ValueError("DOUYIN_REMOTE_CDP_SLOTS 必须是对象")
277:         result: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
278:         for name, value in payload.items():
279:             if (
280:                 not isinstance(name, str)
281:                 or not name.strip()
282:                 or not isinstance(value, dict)
283:             ):
284:                 raise ValueError("远程浏览器槽位配置格式无效")
285:             result[name] = value
286:         return result
287: 
288:     async def _comment_video(
289:         self,
290:         page: Page,
291:         request: InteractionExecutionRequest,
292:         step_callback: InteractionStepCallback | None,
293:     ) -> InteractionExecutionResult:
294:         """执行「评论作品」：打开视频页、展开评论区并定位输入框后填写并提交。"""
295:         await self._open_video(page, request.aweme_id)
296:         await self._trace(step_callback, page, "video_opened", "已打开目标视频页面")
297:         active_page, editor = await self._open_comment_panel(
298:             page, aweme_id=request.aweme_id
299:         )
300:         if editor is None:
301:             raise InteractionExecutionError(
302:                 "comment_not_available",
303:                 "当前作品没有可用的评论输入框，可能已关闭评论",
304:             )
305:         PageController._assert_video_page(active_page, request.aweme_id)
306:         await self._trace(
307:             step_callback,
308:             active_page,
309:             "comment_editor_ready",
310:             "评论区已展开并定位到评论输入框",
311:         )
312:         return await SubmitFlow._fill_and_submit(
313:             active_page,
314:             editor,
315:             request.content,
316:             step_callback,
317:             require_explicit_submit=True,
318:             require_comment_confirmation=True,
319:             expected_aweme_id=request.aweme_id,
320:         )
321:

33: class SubmitFlow:
34:     """互动内容填写与提交确认。
35: 
36:     两个入口方法（``_fill_and_submit`` / ``_wait_comment_submission``）为类方法，
37:     通过协作类组合发送流程；不持有浏览器状态。

40:     @staticmethod
41:     async def _report(
42:         step_callback: InteractionStepCallback | None,

44:         step: str,
45:         detail: str,
46:     ) -> None:
47:         """若提供了步骤回调，则上报一个执行步骤（本类内部专用）。"""
48:         if step_callback is not None:
49:             await step_callback(page, step, detail)

51:     @classmethod

60:         require_explicit_submit: bool = False,
61:         require_comment_confirmation: bool = False,

63:         expected_reply_context: Locator | None = None,
64:         expected_reply_comment_id: str | None = None,
65:         expected_parent_comment_id: str | None = None,
66:     ) -> InteractionExecutionResult:
67:         """填写内容并触发发送，等待抖音接口确认结果。
68: 
69:         参数：
70:             page: 当前页面。
71:             editor: 输入框定位器。
72:             content: 互动文本内容。
73:             step_callback: 步骤回调。
74:             response_markers: 响应 URL 匹配标记，缺省使用私信标记。
75:             require_explicit_submit: 为 True 时找不到发送按钮则直接失败（不用快捷键兜底）。
76:             require_comment_confirmation: 为 True 时必须观测到评论发布请求并得到平台确认。
77:             expected_aweme_id: 期望的作品 ID，发送前校验页面未跳走。
78:             expected_reply_context: 期望的回复评论卡片，发送前校验回复状态仍有效。
79:             expected_reply_comment_id: 期望回复的目标评论 ID，用于核验发布请求的绑定关系。
80:             expected_parent_comment_id: 期望的父评论 ID。
81: 
82:         返回：
83:             互动执行结果。
84: 
85:         异常：
86:             InteractionExecutionError: 各阶段失败时抛出，携带 retryable/ambiguous 等处置标记。
87:         """
88:         try:
89:             await editor.click()
90:             await editor.fill(content)
91:         except Exception as exc:

113:             )
114: 
115:         submit = await PageController._find_submit_control(page, editor)
116:         if require_explicit_submit and submit is None:

120:                 retryable=True,
121:             )
122:         markers = response_markers or MESSAGE_RESPONSE_MARKERS
123:         submitted = False
124:         try:

131:                         response

133:                     timeout=12_000,
134:                 ) as response_info:
135:                     await submit.scroll_into_view_if_needed()
136:                     submitted = await PageController._dispatch_comment_submit(

145:                     await cls._report(

147:                         page,

150:                     )
151:                 response = await response_info.value
152:                 if (

158:                     )
159:                 ):
160:                     raise InteractionExecutionError(

164:                     )
165:                 if not response.ok:
166:                     code = (
167:                         "risk_controlled"

169:                         else "platform_rejected"

171:                     raise InteractionExecutionError(

177:                 status_code = ResponseInspector._platform_status_code(payload)

204:                 await cls._report(

209:                 )
210:                 return InteractionExecutionResult(platform_id=platform_id)
211: 
212:             async with page.expect_response(

214:                 timeout=12_000,
215:             ) as response_info:

239:                 )

306:     @classmethod
307:     async def _wait_comment_submission(
308:         cls,
309:         page: Page,
310:         *,
311:         request_content: str,

313:     ) -> None:
314:         """等待抖音页面给出 UI 层面的判定（发布失败的响应也可能是 HTTP 200）。
315: 
316:         异常：
317:             InteractionExecutionError: 命中风控/失败提示，或超时仍无法判定时抛出。
318:         """
319:         deadline = asyncio.get_running_loop().time() + timeout_ms / 1000
320:         stable_editor = page.locator(

322:         ).first
323:         while True:
324:             success_message = await dom.visible_page_message(
325:                 page, COMMENT_SUCCESS_MESSAGES
326:             )

329:             risk_message = await dom.visible_page_message(

331:             )
332:             if risk_message:
333:                 raise InteractionExecutionError(

336:                     affects_account_health=True,
337:                 )
338:             failure_message = await dom.visible_page_message(
339:                 page, COMMENT_FAILURE_MESSAGES
340:             )
341:             if failure_message:
342:                 raise InteractionExecutionError(
343:                     "platform_rejected",

345:                     affects_account_health=True,
346:                 )

348:                 if await stable_editor.count():

353:                         return
354:                 else:
355:                     root = page.locator("#comment-input-container").first
356:                     root_text = (
357:                         await root.text_content(timeout=300) or ""
358:                         if await root.count()
359:                         else ""
360:                     )
361:                     if request_content.strip() not in root_text.strip():
362:                         return
363:             except InteractionExecutionError:

4: 
5: 负责评论列表激活、按评论 id/内容定位目标评论、打开回复输入框以及私信会话
6: 编辑器的查找；通用 DOM 基元来自 ``crawler.browser.runtime.dom``，选择器见 ``selectors``。

26: class CommentLocator:
27:     """评论目标与私信编辑器的定位器。
28: 
29:     大部分方法为类方法：按需在页面内滚动/点击/核验，从 ``dom``
30:     组合基础操作；本类自身不持有页面状态。
31:     """
32: 
33:     @classmethod
34:     async def _find_comment_target(
35:         cls, page: Page, request: InteractionExecutionRequest
36:     ) -> Locator | None:
37:         """在评论列表中按评论 ID 或内容定位目标评论。
38: 
39:         滚动加载评论列表直至找到唯一匹配项，或确认无法找到。
40: 
41:         返回：
42:             命中的评论节点定位器；未找到返回 None。
43:         """
44:         no_scroll_progress = 0
45:         for _ in range(48):
46:             comment_list = await dom.find_visible(
47:                 page, COMMENT_LIST_SELECTORS, timeout=250
48:             )
49:             roots: tuple[Page | Locator, ...] = (
50:                 (comment_list,) if comment_list is not None else (page,)
51:             )
52:             for root in roots:
53:                 candidates: list[tuple[Locator, bool]] = []
54:                 if request.target_comment_id and request.target_comment_id.isdigit():
55:                     tooltip = root.locator(
56:                         f'[id="tooltip_{request.target_comment_id}"]'
57:                     )
58:                     candidates.extend(
59:                         [
60:                             (
61:                                 tooltip.locator(
62:                                     "xpath=ancestor::*[@data-e2e='comment-item'][1]"
63:                                 ),
64:                                 False,
65:                             ),
66:                             (
67:                                 root.locator(
68:                                     f'[data-comment-id="{request.target_comment_id}"]'
69:                                 ),
70:                                 False,
71:                             ),
72:                             (
73:                                 root.locator(
74:                                     f'[data-cid="{request.target_comment_id}"]'
75:                                 ),
76:                                 False,
77:                             ),
78:                         ]
79:                     )
80:                 assert request.target_comment_content is not None
81:                 candidates.append(
82:                     (
83:                         root.get_by_text(request.target_comment_content, exact=True),
84:                         True,
85:                     )
86:                 )
87:                 if len(request.target_comment_content) > 40:
88:                     candidates.append(
89:                         (
90:                             root.get_by_text(
91:                                 request.target_comment_content[:40], exact=False
92:                             ),
93:                             True,
94:                         )
95:                     )
96:                 for candidates_locator, require_unique in candidates:
97:                     try:
98:                         count = await candidates_locator.count()
99:                         visible: list[Locator] = []
100:                         for index in range(count):
101:                             candidate = candidates_locator.nth(index)
102:                             if await candidate.is_visible():
103:                                 if not require_unique:
104:                                     return candidate
105:                                 visible.append(candidate)
106:                         if len(visible) == 1:
107:                             return visible[0]
108:                     except Exception:
109:                         continue
110:             if await cls._scroll_comment_list(page):
111:                 no_scroll_progress = 0
112:             else:
113:                 no_scroll_progress += 1
114:                 if no_scroll_progress >= 8:
115:                     break
116:             await page.wait_for_timeout(600)
117:         return None

119:     @classmethod
120:     async def _scroll_comment_list(cls, page: Page) -> bool:
121:         """滚动抖音内部的路由级滚动容器（而非 window）。
122: 
123:         返回：

125:         """

128:         )
129:         if scroll_anchor is None:

131:             # 从可见评论节点出发，脚本仍能找到最近的真实滚动祖先容器。
132:             scroll_anchor = await dom.find_visible(
133:                 page, COMMENT_ITEM_SELECTORS, timeout=250
134:             )
135:         return await dom.scroll_container_to_bottom(page, scroll_anchor)

138:     async def _ensure_comment_list_active(cls, page: Page) -> bool:
139:         """确保评论列表处于可见激活状态：必要时点击评论标签并逐方式验证。
140: 
141:         返回：

143:         """
144:         if await cls._find_visible_comment_surface(page, timeout=300) is not None:
145:             return True

148:         )
149:         if control is None:
150:             return False
151:         activators: tuple[Callable[[], Awaitable[None]], ...] = (
152:             lambda: control.dispatch_event("click"),
153:             lambda: control.click(timeout=2_000),
154:             lambda: dom.click_control_center(page, control),
155:         )
156:         for activate in activators:

158:                 await activate()
159:             except Exception:

161:             if await cls._find_visible_comment_surface(page, timeout=1_500) is not None:
162:                 return True

166:     async def _find_visible_comment_surface(
167:         cls, page: Page, *, timeout: int
168:     ) -> Locator | None:
169:         """查找可见的评论列表容器；没有独立列表标记时退化为查找可见评论节点。"""
170:         comment_list = await dom.find_visible(
171:             page, COMMENT_LIST_SELECTORS, timeout=timeout
172:         )

179:     @classmethod
180:     async def _open_reply_editor(
181:         cls,
182:         page: Page,
183:         target: Locator,
184:         request: InteractionExecutionRequest,
185:     ) -> tuple[Locator | None, Locator | None]:
186:         """在目标评论卡片上点击「回复」，等待出现处于回复状态的输入框。
187: 
188:         返回：
189:             (评论卡片, 回复输入框) 二元组；任一步骤失败返回 (None, None)。
190:         """
191:         comment_card = target.locator(
192:             "xpath=ancestor-or-self::*[@data-e2e='comment-item'][1]"
193:         )
194:         try:
195:             if not await comment_card.count() or not await comment_card.is_visible():
196:                 return None, None
197:         except Exception:
198:             return None, None
199:         node = target
200:         reply_control: Locator | None = None
201:         for _ in range(16):
202:             replies = node.get_by_text("回复", exact=True)
203:             visible_replies: list[Locator] = []
204:             for index in range(await replies.count()):
205:                 reply = replies.nth(index)
206:                 if await reply.is_visible():
207:                     visible_replies.append(reply)
208:             if len(visible_replies) == 1:
209:                 reply_control = visible_replies[0]
210:                 break
211:             if len(visible_replies) > 1:
212:                 return None, None
213:             node = node.locator("xpath=..")
214:         if reply_control is None:
215:             return None, None
216: 
217:         activators: tuple[Callable[[], Awaitable[None]], ...] = (
218:             lambda: reply_control.dispatch_event("click"),
219:             lambda: reply_control.click(timeout=2_000),
220:             lambda: dom.click_control_center(page, reply_control),
221:         )
222:         for activate in activators:
223:             try:
224:                 await activate()
225:             except Exception:
226:                 continue
227:             deadline = asyncio.get_running_loop().time() + 2.0
228:             while asyncio.get_running_loop().time() < deadline:
229:                 editor = await dom.find_visible(
230:                     page, COMMENT_EDITOR_SELECTORS, timeout=250
231:                 )
232:                 if editor is not None and await cls._reply_context_is_active(
233:                     comment_card, request.target_comment_id
234:                 ):
235:                     return comment_card, editor
236:                 await page.wait_for_timeout(150)
237:         return None, None
238: 
239:     @staticmethod
240:     async def _reply_context_is_active(
241:         comment_card: Locator, target_comment_id: str | None
242:     ) -> bool:
243:         """校验评论卡片是否处于对目标评论的「回复中」状态。"""
244:         try:
245:             if target_comment_id:
246:                 matches_target = False
247:                 for attribute in ("data-comment-id", "data-cid"):
248:                     if await comment_card.get_attribute(attribute) == target_comment_id:
249:                         matches_target = True
250:                         break
251:                 selectors = (
252:                     f'[id="tooltip_{target_comment_id}"]',
253:                     f'[data-comment-id="{target_comment_id}"]',
254:                     f'[data-cid="{target_comment_id}"]',
255:                 )
256:                 for selector in selectors:
257:                     matches = comment_card.locator(selector)
258:                     for index in range(await matches.count()):
259:                         if await matches.nth(index).is_visible():
260:                             matches_target = True
261:                             break
262:                     if matches_target:
263:                         break
264:                 if not matches_target:
265:                     return False
266:             active = comment_card.get_by_text("回复中", exact=True)
267:             for index in range(await active.count()):
268:                 if await active.nth(index).is_visible():
269:                     return True
270:             return False
271:         except Exception:
272:             return False

276:         cls,

306:             if remaining <= 0:

420:     @staticmethod
421:     async def _lookup_target_comment(
422:         client: DouyinClient, request: InteractionExecutionRequest
423:     ) -> str:
424:         """通过实时评论接口翻页核验目标评论。
425: 
426:         返回：
427:             present（目标存在）/ unavailable（已完整翻页确认不存在）/ inconclusive（无法定论）。
428:         """
429:         if not request.target_comment_id:
430:             return "inconclusive"
431:         cursor = 0
432:         total = 0
433:         seen_cursors: set[int] = set()
434:         for _ in range(50):
435:             try:
436:                 parent_comment_id = request.target_parent_comment_id
437:                 if parent_comment_id not in {None, "", "0"}:
438:                     assert parent_comment_id is not None
439:                     payload = await client.comments_api.get_sub_comments_page(
440:                         request.aweme_id,
441:                         parent_comment_id,
442:                         cursor,
443:                     )
444:                 else:
445:                     payload = await client.comments_api.get_comments_page(request.aweme_id, cursor)
446:             except Exception:
447:                 return "inconclusive"
448:             # 抖音业务状态码非零、或响应缺少分页契约字段，都不能证明评论已消失；
449:             # 统一按 inconclusive 处理，保证任务可安全重试。
450:             if payload.get("status_code") not in (0, "0"):
451:                 return "inconclusive"
452:             if "comments" not in payload or "has_more" not in payload:
453:                 return "inconclusive"
454:             comments = payload["comments"]
455:             if not isinstance(comments, list):
456:                 return "inconclusive"
457:             for comment in comments:
458:                 if str(comment.get("cid") or "") == request.target_comment_id:
459:                     return "present"
460:             total += len(comments)
461:             has_more = payload.get("has_more")
462:             if has_more in (False, 0, "0"):
463:                 return "unavailable"
464:             if has_more not in (True, 1, "1"):
465:                 return "inconclusive"
466:             if not comments or total >= 1_000:
467:                 return "inconclusive"
468:             try:
469:                 next_cursor = int(payload.get("cursor") or 0)
470:             except (TypeError, ValueError):
471:                 return "inconclusive"
472:             if next_cursor == cursor or next_cursor in seen_cursors:
473:                 return "inconclusive"
474:             seen_cursors.add(cursor)
475:             cursor = next_cursor
476:             await asyncio.sleep(0.2)
477:         return "inconclusive"

569:     async def _open_video(self, page: Page, aweme_id: str) -> None:
570:         """打开目标视频页并等待进入可互动状态，失败时按配置次数自动重试。
571: 
572:         异常：
573:             InteractionExecutionError: 网络加载失败或页面持续未就绪时抛出。
574:         """
575:         target_url = f"https://www.douyin.com/video/{quote(aweme_id, safe='')}"
576:         attempts = self.settings.DOUYIN_INTERACTION_NAVIGATION_ATTEMPTS
577:         for attempt in range(attempts):
578:             try:
579:                 await page.goto(
580:                     target_url,
581:                     wait_until="commit",
582:                     timeout=30_000,
583:                 )
584:             except PlaywrightTimeoutError:
585:                 # 文档已 commit 时，导航超时后页面仍会继续渲染。
586:                 pass
587:             except PlaywrightError as exc:
588:                 if attempt + 1 >= attempts:
589:                     raise InteractionExecutionError(
590:                         "page_navigation_failed",
591:                         "目标视频页面网络加载失败，已自动重试仍未恢复",
592:                         retryable=True,
593:                         affects_account_health=True,
594:                     ) from exc
595:                 await page.wait_for_timeout((attempt + 1) * 1_000)
596:                 continue
597: 
598:             try:
599:                 await page.wait_for_function(
600:                     VIDEO_PAGE_READY_SCRIPT,
601:                     timeout=(
602:                         self.settings.DOUYIN_INTERACTION_PAGE_READY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS
603:                         * 1000
604:                     ),
605:                 )
606:                 return
607:             except PlaywrightTimeoutError as exc:
608:                 if attempt + 1 >= attempts:
609:                     raise InteractionExecutionError(
610:                         "page_load_timeout",
611:                         "目标视频页面持续加载，自动刷新后仍未进入可互动状态",
612:                         retryable=True,
613:                     ) from exc
614: 
615:             # 重试目标 URL 前，先丢弃卡在抖音加载壳上的渲染进程。
616:             # 这样恢复动作始终限定在一次已确认的互动尝试内，且不会回退出 CDP 方案。
617:             try:
618:                 await page.goto(
619:                     "about:blank",
620:                     wait_until="commit",
621:                     timeout=5_000,
622:                 )
623:             except PlaywrightError:
624:                 pass
625:             await page.wait_for_timeout((attempt + 1) * 1_000)
626: 
627:     async def _open_comment_panel(
628:         self, page: Page, *, aweme_id: str | None = None
629:     ) -> tuple[Page, Locator | None]:
630:         """在自动化标签页内展开评论区并定位评论输入框。
631: 
632:         返回：
633:             (输入框所在页面, 输入框定位器)；超时未找到时返回 (原页面, None)。
634:         """
635:         deadline = (
636:             asyncio.get_running_loop().time()
637:             + self.settings.DOUYIN_INTERACTION_COMMENT_READY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS
638:         )
639:         control_clicked_pages: set[int] = set()
640:         next_entry_click_at: dict[int, float] = {}
641:         while asyncio.get_running_loop().time() < deadline:
642:             candidates = PageController._interaction_pages(page, aweme_id=aweme_id)
643:             for candidate in candidates:
644:                 editor = await dom.find_visible(
645:                     candidate, COMMENT_EDITOR_SELECTORS, timeout=500
646:                 )
647:                 if editor is not None:
648:                     return candidate, editor
649: 
650:             now = asyncio.get_running_loop().time()
651:             for candidate in candidates:
652:                 page_key = id(candidate)
653:                 if page_key not in control_clicked_pages:
654:                     control = await dom.find_visible(
655:                         candidate, COMMENT_TAB_SELECTORS, timeout=400
656:                     )
657:                     if control is not None:
658:                         editor = await self._activate_comment_control(
659:                             candidate, control
660:                         )
661:                         control_clicked_pages.add(page_key)
662:                         if editor is not None:
663:                             return candidate, editor
664: 
665:                 if now < next_entry_click_at.get(page_key, 0.0):
666:                     continue
667:                 entry = await dom.find_visible(
668:                     candidate, COMMENT_ENTRY_SELECTORS, timeout=400
669:                 )
670:                 if entry is not None:
671:                     editor = await self._activate_comment_control(
672:                         candidate, entry, require_editor=True
673:                     )
674:                     if editor is not None:
675:                         return candidate, editor
676:                     next_entry_click_at[page_key] = now + 2.0
677: 
678:             remaining = deadline - asyncio.get_running_loop().time()
679:             if remaining > 0:
680:                 await page.wait_for_timeout(min(500, int(remaining * 1000)))
681:         return page, None
682: 
683:     async def _activate_comment_control(
684:         self,
685:         page: Page,
686:         control: Locator,
687:         *,
688:         require_editor: bool = False,
689:     ) -> Locator | None:
690:         """激活控件并验证抖音已切换到可编辑状态。
691: 
692:         require_editor 为 False 时，评论区入口展开（但未出现输入框）也视为
693:         阶段性成功并返回 None。
694:         """
695:         activators: tuple[Callable[[], Awaitable[None]], ...] = (
696:             lambda: control.dispatch_event("click"),
697:             lambda: control.click(timeout=2_000),
698:             lambda: dom.click_control_center(page, control),
699:         )
700:         for activate in activators:
701:             try:
702:                 await activate()
703:             except Exception:
704:                 continue
705:             await page.wait_for_timeout(200)
706:             editor = await dom.find_visible(
707:                 page, COMMENT_EDITOR_SELECTORS, timeout=500
708:             )
709:             if editor is not None:
710:                 return editor
711:             if not require_editor:
712:                 entry = await dom.find_visible(
713:                     page, COMMENT_ENTRY_SELECTORS, timeout=300
714:                 )
715:                 if entry is not None:
716:                     return None
717:         return None
718:

1: # Portions adapted from MediaCrawler, NON-COMMERCIAL LEARNING LICENSE 1.1.
2: 
3: """抖音互动页面的 DOM 查询与点击集合（PageController）。
4: 
5: 页面级查询/点击基元（可见元素查找、坐标点击、输入框判空、文案探测）已下沉到
6: ``crawler.browser.runtime.dom``，由 browser 模块统一提供、本模块直接导入。
7: 此处仅保留带抖音语义的操作：评论发布请求的触发与观测、发送控件查找、
8: 页面是否停留在目标视频页的断言，以及互动页面的筛选。
9: """
10: 
11: from __future__ import annotations
12: 
13: import asyncio
14: from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
15: from typing import Any
16: 
17: from crawler.browser.runtime import dom
18: from crawler.douyin_client.base.errors import InteractionExecutionError
19: from crawler.douyin_client.interactions.response_inspector import ResponseInspector
20: from crawler.douyin_client.interactions.selectors import (
21:     COMMENT_SUBMIT_SELECTORS,
22:     MESSAGE_SUBMIT_SELECTORS,
23: )
24: from playwright.async_api import Locator, Page
25: 
26: 
27: class PageController:
28:     """抖音互动页面的 DOM 查询与点击集合。
29: 
30:     所有方法均为静态方法：只依赖传入的页面/定位器与 selectors 常量，
31:     不持有页面状态，便于跨协作类复用。通用基元经 ``dom.*`` 调用。
32:     """
33: 
34:     @staticmethod
35:     async def _dispatch_comment_submit(page: Page, control: Locator) -> bool:
36:         """对抖音变换后的评论发送控件执行且仅执行一次激活。
37: 
38:         返回：
39:             是否观测到了评论发布请求。
40:         """
41:         publish_requested = asyncio.Event()
42: 
43:         def observe_request(request: Any) -> None:
44:             """监听网络请求，命中评论发布接口时置位事件。"""
45:             if ResponseInspector._is_comment_publish_request(request):
46:                 publish_requested.set()
47: 
48:         page.on("request", observe_request)
49:         try:
50:             # 抖音经过变换的评论编辑器可能接受组件级点击；而 Playwright 报告
51:             # 坐标点击成功时，文档实际可能没有收到任何 click 事件。因此先在
52:             # 组件可见时触发 React 组件点击，再以可信的 CDP 坐标点击兜底。
53:             activators: tuple[Callable[[], Awaitable[None]], ...] = (
54:                 lambda: control.dispatch_event("click"),
55:                 lambda: control.click(timeout=5_000),
56:                 lambda: dom.click_control_center(page, control),
57:             )
58:             last_error: Exception | None = None
59:             activated = False
60:             for activate in activators:
61:                 if publish_requested.is_set():
62:                     return True
63:                 try:
64:                     await activate()
65:                     activated = True
66:                 except Exception as exc:
67:                     last_error = exc
68:                     continue
69:                 try:
70:                     await asyncio.wait_for(publish_requested.wait(), timeout=1.5)
71:                     return True
72:                 except TimeoutError:
73:                     continue
74:             if not activated and last_error is not None:
75:                 raise last_error
76:             return publish_requested.is_set()
77:         finally:
78:             page.remove_listener("request", observe_request)
79: 
80:     @staticmethod
81:     async def _find_submit_control(page: Page, editor: Locator) -> Locator | None:
82:         """查找发送按钮：先按选择器直查，再从输入框向上找「发送/发布」按钮，最后全页兜底。"""
83:         direct = await dom.find_visible(
84:             page,
85:             (
86:                 *COMMENT_SUBMIT_SELECTORS,
87:                 *MESSAGE_SUBMIT_SELECTORS,
88:             ),
89:             timeout=1_500,
90:         )
91:         if direct is not None:
92:             return direct
93:         node = editor
94:         for _ in range(6):
95:             for label in ("发送", "发布"):
96:                 control = node.get_by_role("button", name=label, exact=True).first
97:                 try:
98:                     if await control.count() and await control.is_visible():
99:                         return control
100:                 except Exception:
101:                     continue
102:             node = node.locator("xpath=..")
103:         return await dom.find_text_control(page, ("发送", "发布"))
104: 
105:     @staticmethod
106:     def _assert_video_page(page: Page, aweme_id: str) -> None:
107:         """断言页面仍停留在目标视频页，否则抛 page_interrupted 安全终止发送。"""
108:         if page.is_closed() or aweme_id not in page.url:
109:             raise InteractionExecutionError(
110:                 "page_interrupted",
111:                 "自动化专用标签页被关闭或切换，发送前已安全终止；请重试任务",
112:                 retryable=True,
113:             )
114: 
115:     @staticmethod
116:     def _interaction_pages(page: Page, *, aweme_id: str | None) -> list[Page]:
117:         """筛选允许参与互动的页面，绝不让无关的用户标签页参与互动。"""
118:         if page.is_closed():
119:             return []
120:         if aweme_id and aweme_id not in page.url:
121:             return []
122:         return [page]

1: # Portions adapted from MediaCrawler, NON-COMMERCIAL LEARNING LICENSE 1.1.
2: 
3: """评论/私信发布响应的无状态解析（纯静态工具）。
4: 
5: 集中发布请求/响应识别、平台状态码、错误文案与结果 id 提取等判定；
6: 被 ``page_controller``（提交触发）与 ``submit_flow``（发送确认）复用。
7: """
8: 
9: from __future__ import annotations
10: 
11: from typing import Any
12: from urllib.parse import parse_qs
13: 
14: 
15: class ResponseInspector:
16:     """抖音互动网络响应的无状态解析器。
17: 
18:     所有方法均为静态方法：只依赖传入的 request/response/payload，不持有状态，
19:     便于被其他协作类与测试直接复用。
20:     """
21: 
22:     @staticmethod
23:     async def _safe_json(response: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
24:         """尽力解析响应 JSON，解析失败或非对象时返回空字典。"""
25:         try:
26:             payload = await response.json()
27:         except Exception:
28:             return {}
29:         return payload if isinstance(payload, dict) else {}
30: 
31:     @staticmethod
32:     def _is_comment_publish_response(response: Any) -> bool:
33:         """判断响应是否来自抖音（新旧版）评论发布接口。"""
34:         try:
35:             return ResponseInspector._is_comment_publish_request(response.request)
36:         except Exception:
37:             return False
38: 
39:     @staticmethod
40:     def _request_targets_reply(
41:         request: Any,
42:         *,
43:         target_comment_id: str,
44:         parent_comment_id: str | None,
45:     ) -> bool:
46:         """核验评论发布请求确实绑定到了已确认的目标评论（含二级回复场景）。"""
47:         try:
48:             post_data = str(request.post_data or "")
49:             fields: dict[str, list[str]] = parse_qs(post_data, keep_blank_values=True)
50:         except Exception:
51:             return False
52:         reply_id = (fields.get("reply_id") or [""])[0]
53:         reply_to_reply_id = (fields.get("reply_to_reply_id") or [""])[0]
54:         if parent_comment_id not in {None, "", "0"}:
55:             return (
56:                 reply_id == parent_comment_id and reply_to_reply_id == target_comment_id
57:             )
58:         return reply_id == target_comment_id and reply_to_reply_id in {"", "0"}
59: 
60:     @staticmethod
61:     def _is_comment_publish_request(request: Any) -> bool:
62:         """判断请求是否为评论发布请求（POST 且路径含 comment 与 publish/create/post）。"""
63:         try:
64:             if request.method != "POST":
65:                 return False
66:             path = request.url.split("?", 1)[0].lower()
67:         except Exception:
68:             return False
69:         return "comment" in path and any(
70:             marker in path for marker in ("publish", "create", "post")
71:         )
72: 
73:     @staticmethod
74:     def _result_id(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
75:         """从平台响应中提取评论 ID（cid/comment_id，兼容多层包裹），未找到返回 None。"""
76:         for candidate in (payload, payload.get("data"), payload.get("result")):
77:             if not isinstance(candidate, dict):
78:                 continue
79:             nested = candidate.get("comment")
80:             if isinstance(nested, dict):
81:                 value = nested.get("cid") or nested.get("comment_id")
82:                 if value:
83:                     return str(value)
84:             value = candidate.get("cid") or candidate.get("comment_id")
85:             if value:
86:                 return str(value)
87:         return None
88: 
89:     @staticmethod
90:     def _platform_status_code(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> object | None:
91:         """从（新旧版包裹的）响应中读取抖音业务状态码，找不到返回 None。"""
92:         for candidate in (
93:             payload,
94:             payload.get("data"),
95:             payload.get("result"),
96:         ):
97:             if isinstance(candidate, dict) and "status_code" in candidate:
98:                 return candidate.get("status_code")
99:         return None
100: 
101:     @staticmethod
102:     def _platform_error_message(payload: dict[str, Any], *, prefix: str) -> str:
103:         """从响应中提取平台错误文案并拼接前缀，未找到时仅返回前缀。"""
104:         for candidate in (payload, payload.get("data"), payload.get("result")):
105:             if not isinstance(candidate, dict):
106:                 continue
107:             for key in ("status_msg", "message", "description"):
108:                 value = candidate.get(key)
109:                 if isinstance(value, str) and value.strip():
110:                     return f"{prefix}：{value.strip()}"
111:         return prefix

(End of file - total 111 lines)
</content>

102: VIDEO_PAGE_READY_SCRIPT = """() => {
103:     const bodyText = document.body?.innerText || '';
104:     const stillLoading = bodyText.includes('视频数据加载中');
105:     const hasVideo = document.querySelectorAll('video').length > 0;
106:     const hasInteractionUi = Boolean(document.querySelector(
107:         '#comment-input-container, .comment-input-inner-container, '
108:         + '[data-e2e="feed-comment-icon"], [data-e2e="comment-icon"]'
109:     ));
110:     return !stillLoading
111:         && (hasVideo || hasInteractionUi);
112: }"""

1: # Portions adapted from MediaCrawler, NON-COMMERCIAL LEARNING LICENSE 1.1.
2: 
3: """抖音登录流程封装：cookie 登录与扫码登录（基于 Playwright 浏览器上下文）。"""
4: 
5: import asyncio
6: import base64
7: import logging
8: import re
9: from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
10: from pathlib import Path
11: 
12: import httpx
13: from crawler.browser.runtime.cookies import parse_cookie_string
14: from crawler.douyin_client.base.errors import LoginError
15: from crawler.douyin_client.http.client import DouyinClient
16: from playwright.async_api import BrowserContext, Page
17: 
18: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
19: # 二维码更新回调：参数为二维码图片路径，None 表示二维码已清除
20: QRCodeCallback = Callable[[Path | None], Awaitable[None]]
21: 
22: 
23: class DouyinLogin:
24:     """抖音登录器，封装 cookie 登录与扫码登录两种流程。
25: 
26:     通过 Playwright 浏览器上下文写入 cookie 或引导用户扫码，
27:     登录成功后回写 DouyinClient 的 cookies。
28: 
29:     参数：
30:         browser_context: Playwright 浏览器上下文。
31:         page: 已打开抖音站点的页面。
32:         qrcode_path: 登录二维码图片的落盘路径。
33:         timeout: 扫码登录的等待超时时间（秒）。
34:         on_qrcode: 二维码更新回调，收到图片路径或 None（清除）。
35:     """
36: 
37:     def __init__(
38:         self,
39:         *,
40:         browser_context: BrowserContext,
41:         page: Page,
42:         qrcode_path: Path,
43:         timeout: float,
44:         on_qrcode: QRCodeCallback,
45:     ):
46:         self.browser_context = browser_context
47:         self.page = page
48:         self.qrcode_path = qrcode_path
49:         self.timeout = timeout
50:         self.on_qrcode = on_qrcode
51: 
52:     async def login_with_cookie(self, cookie_string: str, client: DouyinClient) -> None:
53:         """使用 cookie 字符串完成登录。
54: 
55:         清空 douyin.com 域的旧 cookie 后写入新 cookie，
56:         刷新页面并调用接口校验登录状态。
57: 
58:         参数：
59:             cookie_string: 抖音站点的 cookie 字符串。
60:             client: 用于同步 cookie 与校验登录状态的客户端。
61: 
62:         异常：
63:             LoginError: cookie 为空、无效或已过期时抛出。
64:         """
65:         cookie_items = parse_cookie_string(cookie_string)
66:         if not cookie_items:
67:             raise LoginError("cookie 登录需要非空 cookies")
68:         await self.browser_context.clear_cookies(
69:             domain=re.compile(r"(^|\.)douyin\.com$")
70:         )
71:         await self.browser_context.add_cookies(
72:             [
73:                 {"name": key, "value": value, "domain": ".douyin.com", "path": "/"}
74:                 for key, value in cookie_items.items()
75:             ]
76:         )
77:         await self.page.reload(wait_until="domcontentloaded")
78:         await client.update_cookies(self.browser_context)
79:         if not await client.pong(self.browser_context):
80:             raise LoginError("提供的抖音 cookies 无效或已过期")
81: 
82:     async def login_with_qrcode(
83:         self, client: DouyinClient, *, require_self_profile: bool
84:     ) -> None:
85:         """扫码登录：弹出登录框、持续抓取二维码并等待用户扫码完成。
86: 
87:         参数：
88:             client: 用于检测登录状态与回写 cookie 的客户端。
89:             require_self_profile: 是否要求通过本人资料接口校验登录状态。
90: 
91:         异常：
92:             LoginError: 超时仍未完成扫码登录时抛出。
93:         """
94:         await self._popup_login_dialog()
95:         selector = "xpath=//div[@id='animate_qrcode_container']//img"
96:         last_source = ""
97:         deadline = asyncio.get_running_loop().time() + self.timeout
98:         while asyncio.get_running_loop().time() < deadline:
99:             if await client.pong(
100:                 self.browser_context, require_self_profile=require_self_profile
101:             ):
102:                 await client.update_cookies(self.browser_context)
103:                 await self._clear_qrcode()
104:                 return
105:             source = await self._qrcode_source(selector)
106:             if source and source != last_source:
107:                 await self._save_qrcode(source)
108:                 last_source = source
109:             await asyncio.sleep(1)
110:         await self._clear_qrcode()
111:         raise LoginError("等待抖音扫码登录超时")
112: 
113:     async def _popup_login_dialog(self) -> None:
114:         """打开抖音登录弹窗；按候选控件依次尝试点击「登录」，全部失败时抛 LoginError。"""
115:         selector = "xpath=//div[@id='login-panel-new']"
116:         try:
117:             await self.page.wait_for_selector(selector, timeout=5_000)
118:             return
119:         except Exception:
120:             pass
121: 
122:         # 抖音当前版本的页头把「登录」文案包在 button 里。若直接选择第一个
123:         # 匹配的 <p>，可能命中隐藏的用户菜单节点，导致弹窗静默打不开，
124:         # 在有头的 Docker 浏览器中尤其明显。
125:         candidates = [
126:             self.page.get_by_role("button", name="登录", exact=True).first,
127:             self.page.locator("#douyin-header button").filter(has_text="登录").first,
128:             self.page.locator("xpath=//p[normalize-space()='登录']").first,
129:         ]
130:         last_error: Exception | None = None
131:         for button in candidates:
132:             try:
133:                 await button.click(timeout=5_000)
134:             except Exception as click_error:
135:                 last_error = click_error
136:                 try:
137:                     await button.dispatch_event("click")
138:                 except Exception as dispatch_error:
139:                     last_error = dispatch_error
140:                     continue
141:             try:
142:                 await self.page.wait_for_selector(selector, timeout=10_000)
143:                 return
144:             except Exception as dialog_error:
145:                 last_error = dialog_error
146: 
147:         raise LoginError("无法打开抖音登录窗口") from last_error
148: 
149:     async def _qrcode_source(self, selector: str) -> str:
150:         """等待并读取登录二维码图片的 src；超时或异常时返回空字符串。"""
151:         try:
152:             element = await self.page.wait_for_selector(selector, timeout=5_000)
153:             if element is None:
154:                 return ""
155:             return str(await element.get_attribute("src") or "")
156:         except Exception:
157:             return ""
158: 
159:     async def _save_qrcode(self, source: str) -> None:
160:         """将二维码内容（data URL / http(s) 链接 / base64）解码保存到本地并触发回调。
161: 
162:         异常：
163:             LoginError: 无法解析二维码内容时抛出。
164:         """
165:         if source.startswith("data:"):
166:             encoded = source.split(",", 1)[1]
167:             content = base64.b64decode(encoded)
168:         elif source.startswith(("http://", "https://")):
169:             async with httpx.AsyncClient(
170:                 trust_env=False, follow_redirects=True
171:             ) as client:
172:                 response = await client.get(source)
173:                 response.raise_for_status()
174:                 content = response.content
175:         else:
176:             try:
177:                 content = base64.b64decode(source)
178:             except ValueError as exc:
179:                 raise LoginError("无法解析抖音登录二维码") from exc
180:         self.qrcode_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
181:         await asyncio.to_thread(self.qrcode_path.write_bytes, content)
182:         await self.on_qrcode(self.qrcode_path)
183:         logger.info("Douyin login QR code updated: %s", self.qrcode_path)
184: 
185:     async def _clear_qrcode(self) -> None:
186:         """清除二维码：通知回调（None）并删除本地二维码文件。"""
187:         await self.on_qrcode(None)
188:         if self.qrcode_path.exists():
189:             await asyncio.to_thread(self.qrcode_path.unlink, missing_ok=True)

(End of file - total 189 lines)

1: # Portions adapted from MediaCrawler under NON-COMMERCIAL LEARNING LICENSE 1.1.
2: 
3: """抖音 Web 端签名工具：webid 生成与 a_bogus 签名计算。
4: 
5: 签名通过 execjs 调用内置的 resources/douyin.js 完成。
6: """
7: 
8: import random
9: from functools import lru_cache
10: from pathlib import Path
11: 
12: import execjs  # type: ignore[import-untyped]
13: 
14: 
15: def get_web_id() -> str:
16:     """生成抖音 Web 端请求参数 webid（19 位随机标识，模拟浏览器指纹）。"""
17: 
18:     def encode(value: int | None) -> str:
19:         if value is not None:
20:             return str(value ^ (int(16 * random.random()) >> (value // 4)))
21:         return f"{int(1e7)}-{int(1e3)}-{int(4e3)}-{int(8e3)}-{int(1e11)}"
22: 
23:     raw = "".join(encode(int(char)) if char in "018" else char for char in encode(None))
24:     return raw.replace("-", "")[:19]
25: 
26: 
27: @lru_cache(maxsize=1)
28: def _signer() -> execjs.ExternalRuntime.Context:
29:     """加载并编译内置 douyin.js 签名脚本，进程内只编译一次。"""
30:     script_path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "resources" / "douyin.js"
31:     return execjs.compile(script_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
32: 
33: 
34: def get_a_bogus(uri: str, query_string: str, user_agent: str) -> str:
35:     """计算抖音接口的 a_bogus 签名参数。
36: 
37:     参数：
38:         uri: 请求路径，用于选择签名函数（包含 /reply 时使用 sign_reply，否则 sign_datail）。
39:         query_string: 已编码的查询字符串。
40:         user_agent: 请求使用的 User-Agent。
41: 
42:     返回：
43:         a_bogus 签名字符串。
44:     """
45:     function_name = "sign_reply" if "/reply" in uri else "sign_datail"
46:     return str(_signer().call(function_name, query_string, user_agent))

(End of file - total 46 lines)

3: """抖音客户端的异常类型定义：采集、登录与互动执行流程的统一错误层级。"""

1: # Portions adapted from MediaCrawler, NON-COMMERCIAL LEARNING LICENSE 1.1.
2: 
3: """互动执行的数据模型：请求/结果/连接参数与旧版账号访问协议。
4: 
5: 纯数据结构与结构化协议集中于此；页面操作实现见同目录的
6: ``response_inspector`` / ``page_controller`` / ``comment_locator`` / ``submit_flow``，
7: 编排与 CDP 连接解析见 ``executor``。互动执行异常定义在 ``base.errors``。
8: """
9: 
10: from __future__ import annotations
11: 
12: from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
13: from dataclasses import dataclass
14: from pathlib import Path
15: from typing import Protocol
16: 
17: from playwright.async_api import Page
18: 
19: # 互动步骤回调：参数为当前页面、步骤标识与步骤说明
20: InteractionStepCallback = Callable[[Page, str, str], Awaitable[None]]
21: 
22: 
23: class _LegacyAccountId(Protocol):
24:     """旧版账号 ID 的结构化协议（仅要求 int 属性）。"""
25: 
26:     int: int  # 账号整数 ID
27: 
28: 
29: class _LegacyInteractionAccount(Protocol):
30:     """旧版互动账号对象的结构化协议，仅声明本模块需要的属性。"""
31: 
32:     id: _LegacyAccountId  # 账号 ID
33:     browser_mode: object  # 浏览器模式（枚举或其原始值）
34:     profile_key: str  # 本地 profile 目录名
35:     remote_slot: str | None  # 远程浏览器槽位名，None 表示使用默认远程配置
36: 
37: 
38: @dataclass(frozen=True)
39: class InteractionExecutionRequest:
40:     """互动执行请求参数。
41: 
42:     属性：
43:         interaction_type: 互动类型：video_comment（评论作品）/ comment_reply（回复评论）/ 其他为私信作者。
44:         aweme_id: 目标作品 ID。
45:         content: 互动文本内容。
46:         target_comment_id: 目标评论 ID（comment_reply 时使用）。
47:         target_comment_content: 目标评论内容，用于页面内定位。
48:         target_parent_comment_id: 目标评论的父评论 ID（回复二级评论时使用）。
49:     """
50: 
51:     interaction_type: str  # 互动类型：video_comment / comment_reply / 私信作者
52:     aweme_id: str  # 目标作品 ID
53:     content: str  # 互动文本内容
54:     target_comment_id: str | None = None  # 目标评论 ID
55:     target_comment_content: str | None = None  # 目标评论内容，用于页面内定位
56:     target_parent_comment_id: str | None = None  # 目标评论的父评论 ID
57: 
58: 
59: @dataclass(frozen=True)
60: class InteractionExecutionResult:

62: 
63:     属性：
64:         platform_id: 平台侧返回的评论 ID 等标识，未能解析时为 None。
65:     """
66: 
67:     platform_id: str | None = None  # 平台侧返回的评论 ID 等标识
68: 
69: 
70: @dataclass(frozen=True)
71: class InteractionBrowserConnection:

1: # Portions adapted from MediaCrawler under NON-COMMERCIAL LEARNING LICENSE 1.1.
2: 
3: """抖音评论读接口客户端（按业务场景拆分）。
4: 
5: 承载一级/子评论分页与整批抓取；底层带签名的 GET 请求由注入的 ``DouyinClient`` 提供。
6: """
7: 
8: from __future__ import annotations
9: 
10: import asyncio
11: import copy
12: from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
13: from urllib.parse import quote
14: 
15: from crawler.douyin_client.http.request_log import (
16:     CommentCallback,
17:     IntervalProvider,
18:     _interval_seconds,
19: )
20: 
21: if TYPE_CHECKING:
22:     from crawler.douyin_client.http.client import DouyinClient
23: 
24: 
25: class CommentsApi:
26:     """抖音评论读接口客户端。"""
27: 
28:     def __init__(self, client: DouyinClient) -> None:
29:         self._client = client
30: 
31:     async def get_comments_page(
32:         self, aweme_id: str, cursor: int, keyword: str = ""
33:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
34:         """获取作品一级评论分页（/aweme/v1/web/comment/list/）。
35: 
36:         参数：
37:             aweme_id: 作品 ID。
38:             cursor: 分页游标。
39:             keyword: 来源搜索关键词，仅用于构造 Referer。
40: 
41:         返回：
42:             评论接口原始响应 JSON（含 comments、cursor、has_more）。
43:         """
44:         headers = copy.copy(self._client.headers)
45:         headers["Referer"] = quote(
46:             f"https://www.douyin.com/search/{keyword}?type=general", safe=":/"
47:         )
48:         return await self._client.get(
49:             "/aweme/v1/web/comment/list/",
50:             {"aweme_id": aweme_id, "cursor": cursor, "count": 20, "item_type": 0},
51:             headers,
52:         )
53: 
54:     async def get_sub_comments_page(
55:         self, aweme_id: str, comment_id: str, cursor: int, keyword: str = ""
56:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
57:         """获取某条评论的子评论（回复）分页（/aweme/v1/web/comment/list/reply/）。
58: 
59:         参数：
60:             aweme_id: 作品 ID。
61:             comment_id: 一级评论 ID。
62:             cursor: 分页游标。
63:             keyword: 来源搜索关键词，仅用于构造 Referer。
64: 
65:         返回：
66:             子评论接口原始响应 JSON。
67:         """
68:         headers = copy.copy(self._client.headers)
69:         headers["Referer"] = quote(
70:             f"https://www.douyin.com/search/{keyword}?type=general", safe=":/"
71:         )
72:         return await self._client.get(
73:             "/aweme/v1/web/comment/list/reply/",
74:             {
75:                 "comment_id": comment_id,
76:                 "cursor": cursor,
77:                 "count": 20,
78:                 "item_type": 0,
79:                 "item_id": aweme_id,
80:             },
81:             headers,
82:         )
83: 
84:     async def get_all_comments(
85:         self,
86:         aweme_id: str,
87:         *,
88:         interval: IntervalProvider,
89:         include_sub_comments: bool,
90:         callback: CommentCallback,
91:         max_count: int,
92:         keyword: str = "",
93:     ) -> int:
94:         """抓取作品全部评论（含可选子评论），按批次回调给调用方。
95: 
96:         参数：
97:             aweme_id: 作品 ID。
98:             interval: 翻页请求间隔（秒），可为固定值或可调用对象。
99:             include_sub_comments: 是否同时抓取有回复的一级评论的子评论。
100:             callback: 评论批次回调。
101:             max_count: 抓取评论总数上限（含子评论）。
102:             keyword: 来源搜索关键词，仅用于构造 Referer。
103: 
104:         返回：
105:             实际抓取的评论总数。
106:         """
107:         total = 0
108:         cursor = 0
109:         has_more = True
110:         seen_cursors: set[int] = set()
111:         while has_more and total < max_count:
112:             response = await self.get_comments_page(aweme_id, cursor, keyword)
113:             comments = response.get("comments") or []
114:             if not isinstance(comments, list) or not comments:
115:                 break
116:             comments = comments[: max_count - total]
117:             await callback(aweme_id, comments)
118:             total += len(comments)
119:             if include_sub_comments and total < max_count:
120:                 for comment in comments:
121:                     if int(comment.get("reply_comment_total") or 0) > 0:
122:                         total += await self._get_sub_comments(
123:                             aweme_id,
124:                             str(comment.get("cid") or ""),
125:                             keyword,
126:                             interval,
127:                             callback,
128:                             max_count - total,
129:                         )
130:                         if total >= max_count:
131:                             break
132:             has_more = response.get("has_more") in (True, 1, "1")
133:             next_cursor = int(response.get("cursor") or 0)
134:             if not has_more or next_cursor in seen_cursors or next_cursor == cursor:
135:                 break
136:             seen_cursors.add(cursor)
137:             cursor = next_cursor
138:             await asyncio.sleep(_interval_seconds(interval))
139:         return total
140: 
141:     async def _get_sub_comments(
142:         self,
143:         aweme_id: str,
144:         comment_id: str,
145:         keyword: str,
146:         interval: IntervalProvider,
147:         callback: CommentCallback,
148:         max_count: int,
149:     ) -> int:
150:         """分页抓取某条一级评论的子评论并按批回调，返回实际抓取数。"""
151:         if not comment_id or max_count <= 0:
152:             return 0
153:         total = 0
154:         cursor = 0
155:         while total < max_count:
156:             response = await self.get_sub_comments_page(
157:                 aweme_id, comment_id, cursor, keyword
158:             )
159:             comments = response.get("comments") or []
160:             if not isinstance(comments, list) or not comments:
161:                 break
162:             comments = comments[: max_count - total]
163:             await callback(aweme_id, comments)
164:             total += len(comments)
165:             if response.get("has_more") not in (True, 1, "1"):
166:                 break
167:             next_cursor = int(response.get("cursor") or 0)
168:             if next_cursor == cursor:
169:                 break
170:             cursor = next_cursor
171:             await asyncio.sleep(_interval_seconds(interval))
172:         return total

1: # Portions adapted from MediaCrawler, NON-COMMERCIAL LEARNING LICENSE 1.1.
2: 
3: """隐私脱敏与字段映射工具。
4: 
5: 对用户标识做哈希匿名化、昵称打码，
6: 并把抖音接口原始数据映射为内部存储结构（作品、评论）。
7: """
8: 
9: import hashlib
10: import hmac
11: from typing import Any
12: 
13: 
14: def anonymize_user_id(user_id: Any) -> str:
15:     """将用户 ID（uid/sec_uid）做 SHA-256 哈希并截取前 16 位，空输入返回空字符串。"""
16:     normalized = str(user_id or "").strip()
17:     return hashlib.sha256(normalized.encode()).hexdigest()[:16] if normalized else ""
18: 
19: 
20: def anonymize_account_id(account_id: Any, secret_key: str) -> str:
21:     """以 secret_key 为密钥对账号 ID 做 HMAC-SHA256 匿名化，截取前 32 位。
22: 
23:     参数：
24:         account_id: 原始账号 ID。
25:         secret_key: HMAC 密钥。
26: 
27:     返回：
28:         匿名化后的标识；空输入返回空字符串。
29:     """
30:     normalized = str(account_id or "").strip()
31:     if not normalized:
32:         return ""
33:     return hmac.new(
34:         secret_key.encode(), normalized.encode(), hashlib.sha256
35:     ).hexdigest()[:32]
36: 
37: 
38: def mask_nickname(nickname: Any) -> str:
39:     """对昵称打码：保留首尾字符、中间以星号替代；长度不超过 1 时整体打码。"""
40:     value = str(nickname or "")
41:     if len(value) <= 1:
42:         return "*" if value else ""
43:     if len(value) == 2:
44:         return value[0] + "*"
45:     return value[0] + "***" + value[-1]
46: 
47: 
48: def _as_int(value: Any) -> int:
49:     """尽力将值转换为 int，无法转换时返回 0。"""
50:     try:
51:         return int(value or 0)
52:     except (TypeError, ValueError):
53:         return 0
54: 
55: 
56: def _note_images(item: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
57:     """提取图文作品每张图片的下载 URL（取 url_list 最后一项）。"""
58:     result: list[str] = []
59:     for image in item.get("images") or []:
60:         urls = image.get("url_list") or []
61:         if urls:
62:             result.append(str(urls[-1]))
63:     return result
64: 
65: 
66: def _comment_images(item: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
67:     """提取评论图片的下载 URL（取 origin_url.url_list 最后一项）。"""
68:     result: list[str] = []
69:     for image in item.get("image_list") or []:
70:         urls = (image.get("origin_url") or {}).get("url_list") or []
71:         if urls:
72:             result.append(str(urls[-1]))
73:     return result
74: 
75: 
76: def map_aweme(item: dict[str, Any], source_keyword: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
77:     """将抖音作品原始数据映射为内部存储结构（用户字段已脱敏）。
78: 
79:     参数：
80:         item: 抖音接口返回的单个作品（aweme）原始字典。
81:         source_keyword: 采集该作品时使用的搜索关键词。
82: 
83:     返回：
84:         映射后的作品字段字典。
85:     """
86:     aweme_id = str(item.get("aweme_id") or "")
87:     author = item.get("author") or {}
88:     stats = item.get("statistics") or {}
89:     video = item.get("video") or {}
90:     cover_urls = (video.get("raw_cover") or video.get("origin_cover") or {}).get(
91:         "url_list"
92:     ) or []
93:     video_urls = (
94:         (video.get("play_addr_h264") or {}).get("url_list")
95:         or (video.get("play_addr_256") or {}).get("url_list")
96:         or (video.get("play_addr") or {}).get("url_list")
97:         or []
98:     )
99:     music_url = ((item.get("music") or {}).get("play_url") or {}).get("uri") or ""
100:     return {
101:         "aweme_id": aweme_id,
102:         "aweme_type": str(item.get("aweme_type") or ""),
103:         "title": str(item.get("desc") or ""),
104:         "description": str(item.get("desc") or ""),
105:         "create_time": _as_int(item.get("create_time")) or None,
106:         "creator_hash": anonymize_user_id(author.get("uid")),
107:         "sec_uid": anonymize_user_id(author.get("sec_uid")),
108:         "creator_real_sec_uid": str(author.get("sec_uid") or ""),
109:         "nickname": mask_nickname(author.get("nickname")),
110:         "liked_count": _as_int(stats.get("digg_count")),
111:         "collected_count": _as_int(stats.get("collect_count")),
112:         "comment_count": _as_int(stats.get("comment_count")),
113:         "share_count": _as_int(stats.get("share_count")),
114:         "aweme_url": f"https://www.douyin.com/video/{aweme_id}",
115:         "cover_url": str(cover_urls[-1]) if cover_urls else "",
116:         "video_download_url": str(video_urls[-1]) if video_urls else "",
117:         "music_download_url": str(music_url),
118:         "note_download_url": ",".join(_note_images(item)),
119:         "source_keyword": source_keyword,
120:     }
121: 
122: 
123: def map_comment(item: dict[str, Any], aweme_id: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
124:     """将抖音评论原始数据映射为内部存储结构（用户字段已脱敏）。
125: 
126:     参数：
127:         item: 抖音接口返回的单个评论原始字典。
128:         aweme_id: 期望归属的作品 ID，用于校验评论归属。
129: 
130:     返回：
131:         映射后的评论字段字典；评论不属于该作品或缺少评论 ID 时返回 None。
132:     """
133:     if str(item.get("aweme_id") or "") != aweme_id:
134:         return None
135:     user = item.get("user") or {}
136:     comment_id = str(item.get("cid") or "")
137:     if not comment_id:
138:         return None
139:     return {
140:         "comment_id": comment_id,
141:         "aweme_id": aweme_id,
142:         "parent_comment_id": str(item.get("reply_id") or "0"),
143:         "content": str(item.get("text") or ""),
144:         "create_time": _as_int(item.get("create_time")) or None,
145:         "creator_hash": anonymize_user_id(user.get("uid")),
146:         "sec_uid": anonymize_user_id(user.get("sec_uid")),
147:         "nickname": mask_nickname(user.get("nickname")),
148:         "sub_comment_count": _as_int(item.get("reply_comment_total")),
149:         "like_count": _as_int(item.get("digg_count")),
150:         "pictures": ",".join(_comment_images(item)),
151:     }

1: # Portions adapted from MediaCrawler under NON-COMMERCIAL LEARNING LICENSE 1.1.
2: 
3: """抖音请求的公共辅助类型。
4: 
5: 集中 ``DouyinRequestLogEntry`` / ``RequestLogCallback`` 等记录类型与请求间隔解析；
6: ``http/client.py`` 从本模块导入，保持客户端聚焦请求本身。
7: cookie 序列化/解析工具已下沉到 ``crawler.browser.runtime.cookies``。
